     ,-[apps/web/src/features/renewals/columns.tsx:278:24]
 277 |     
 278 | ,-> const UsageTrendCell = memo(function UsageTrendCell({
 279 | |     row,
 280 | |   }: {
 281 | |     row: Row<DataGridFeatures, IRenewalRecord>;
 282 | |   }) {
 283 | |     const data = row.original.usageTrend;
 284 | |     const width = 92;
 285 | |     const height = 26;
 286 | |     const padX = 2;
 287 | |     const padY = 2;
 288 | |     const innerW = width - padX * 2;
 289 | |     const innerH = height - padY * 2;
 290 | |   
 291 | |     const { linePath, strokeClass, deltaLabel } = useMemo(() => {
 292 | |       const max = Math.max(...data);
 293 | |       const min = Math.min(...data);
 294 | |       const range = max - min || 1;
 295 | |       const step = innerW / Math.max(1, data.length - 1);
 296 | |   
 297 | |       const points = data.map((value, index) => ({
 298 | |         x: padX + index * step,
 299 | |         y: padY + ((max - value) / range) * innerH,
 300 | |       }));
 301 | |   
 302 | |       const delta = data[data.length - 1] - data[0];
 303 | |   
 304 | |       return {
 305 | |         linePath: buildSmoothLinePath(points),
 306 | |         strokeClass:
 307 | |           delta > 0
 308 | |             ? "stroke-emerald-600 dark:stroke-emerald-400"
 309 | |             : delta < 0
 310 | |               ? "stroke-red-600 dark:stroke-red-400"
 311 | |               : "stroke-muted-foreground",
 312 | |         deltaLabel:
 313 | |           delta > 0
 314 | |             ? `Expanding ${delta} pts`
 315 | |             : delta < 0
 316 | |               ? `Contracting ${Math.abs(delta)} pts`
 317 | |               : "Stable",
